  setup_complete
    ✓ snake_case setup_complete triggers ctx.onSetupComplete and returns (no other branch runs) (7 ms)
    ✓ camelCase setupComplete is recognized identically (3 ms)
    ✓ arms a 25s upstream ping interval that calls ws.ping() while OPEN (8 ms)
    ✓ does not ping once the socket is no longer OPEN (4 ms)
    ✓ arms the silence keepalive: idle + model-not-speaking feeds a silence frame via deps.sendAudioToLiveAPI (3 ms)
    ✓ silence keepalive does NOT fire while the model is speaking (3 ms)
  server_content — interrupted

    ✓ camelCase serverContent.interrupted is recognized identically (3 ms)
    ✓ grounding_metadata.interrupted (nested alt path) also triggers interruption (4 ms)
    ✓ returns immediately — turn_complete in the same content object never also fires (3 ms)
  server_content — turn_complete
    ✓ snake_case turn_complete: clears watchdog, ungates mic, bumps counters, fires onTurnComplete (20 ms)
    ✓ camelCase turnComplete is recognized identically (4 ms)
    ✓ flushes accumulated input/output transcript buffers into transcriptTurns and clears them (15 ms)
    ✓ greeting turn (turn 1 immediately after greetingSent) skips the user-text write/push entirely (7 ms)
    ✓ writes the user transcript to memory_items when identity is present and text is long enough (5 ms)
    ✓ falls back to DEV_IDENTITY in dev-sandbox mode when no session identity is set (12 ms)
    ✓ does NOT write short (<=20 char) user transcripts to memory (5 ms)
    ✓ closes the upstream WS for a pending persona swap and flags the session (7 ms)
    ✓ does not close upstream for a pending swap when there is no upstreamWs (5 ms)
    ✓ anonymous session hits the hard turn-limit (>8) and sends session_limit_reached (5 ms)
